DEG source      : <SEA-AD>/[1]_NeoC/Part5_NeoC_DEGs/DEG_master_results.xlsx
Mic.13 list     : 95 genes (Green et al. 2024, Extended Data Fig 2c)
Scorer genes    : 24 (9 MT module + 15 MIC13 panel)

  In-scorer (circular) : 14 genes
  Held-out (real test) : 81 genes

TEST 1 - Mic.13 held-out genes among Part 5 sig DEGs:
  All DEGs : 32/81 recovered, fold=4.33x, p=1.3289e-13
  UP DEGs  : 10/81 recovered, fold=3.72x, p=3.1984e-04

TEST 2 - DAM2 signature (Keren-Shaul et al. 2017):
  All DEGs : 6/14 recovered, fold=4.7x, p=8.9307e-04

INTERPRETATION:
  Significant enrichment in TEST 1 (held-out genes) means the M4-top PVM_3
  cells express the same non-scoring Mic.13 markers at elevated levels,
  which is independent evidence of equivalence.

  Significant enrichment in TEST 2 (DAM2) means the cells also recapitulate
  the disease-associated microglia signature from Keren-Shaul et al.

  TEST 3 (pathway enrichment via Part10g R pipeline) is prepared but not
  computed here. Run Part10g on the Part 5 DEG Excel to complete it.
